  const baseManageCovert128 = TacticalAIClass.prototype.manageCovertSquad;
  TacticalAIClass.prototype.manageCovertSquad = function(squad, units) {
    const agent = units[0];
    if (!agent?.alive) {
      squad.unitIds = [];
      return;
    }
    const target = this.game.getEntity(squad.targetId);
    if (target?.alive && target.kind === 'building' && target.team === 'player') {
      return baseManageCovert128.call(this, squad, units);
    }
    const reachedLastKnown = Math.hypot(agent.x - squad.targetX, agent.y - squad.targetY) < 380;
    if (!reachedLastKnown || !this.canConfirmArea128(squad.targetX, squad.targetY)) {
      if (!agent.currentCommand || agent.currentCommand.type === 'infiltrate') {
        agent.setCommand({ type: 'move', x: squad.targetX, y: squad.targetY });
        squad.lastOrderAt = this.game.time;
      }
      return;
    }

    this.intel.delete(squad.targetId);
    const replacement = this.chooseCovertTarget(squad.mission || 'sabotage');
    if (!replacement) {
      this.releaseSquad(squad, true);
      return;
    }
    squad.targetId = replacement.id;
    squad.targetX = replacement.x;
    squad.targetY = replacement.y;
    const path = this.buildFlankPath(replacement, squad.flank || 1, 1.04);
    path.slice(0, -1).forEach((point, index) => agent.setCommand({ type: 'move', x: point.x, y: point.y }, index > 0));
    agent.setCommand({ type: 'infiltrate', targetId: replacement.id, mission: squad.mission || 'sabotage', progress: 0 }, true);
    squad.lastOrderAt = this.game.time;
  };

function makeSectors(game){
  const cols=3,rows=3,arr=[];
  for(let y=0;y<rows;y++)for(let x=0;x<cols;x++){
    const id=`S${y*cols+x+1}`; const x0=x*WORLD.width/cols,y0=y*WORLD.height/rows,x1=(x+1)*WORLD.width/cols,y1=(y+1)*WORLD.height/rows;
    arr.push({id,index:y*cols+x,x0,y0,x1,y1,x:(x0+x1)/2,y:(y0+y1)/2,control:0,owner:'neutral',value:1,playerPower:0,enemyPower:0,resourceValue:0,infraValue:0,roadValue:0,lastChanged:0});
  }
  for(const s of arr){
    for(const r of game.resources||[]) if(r.x>=s.x0&&r.x<s.x1&&r.y>=s.y0&&r.y<s.y1) s.resourceValue+=Math.min(5,(Number(r.amount)||100000)/150000);
    for(const b of game.buildings||[]) if(b.x>=s.x0&&b.x<s.x1&&b.y>=s.y0&&b.y<s.y1) s.infraValue+=b.team==='neutral'?1.5:0.4;
    for(const road of game.roads||[]) for(const p of road||[]) if(p.x>=s.x0&&p.x<s.x1&&p.y>=s.y0&&p.y<s.y1){s.roadValue+=0.18;break;}
    s.value=1+s.resourceValue*0.75+s.infraValue+s.roadValue;
  }
  return arr;
}

function ensureUnitState(u){
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.suppression160))u.suppression160=0;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.cohesion160))u.cohesion160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.supply160))u.supply160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.morale160))u.morale160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.lastSuppressedAt160))u.lastSuppressedAt160=-999;
  if(!u.functionalDamage160)u.functionalDamage160={engine:0,sensors:0,weapon:0};
  return u;
}
function detectionRadius(e){return Math.max(Number(e?.detector)||Number(e?.stats?.detector)||0,Number(e?.vision)||Number(e?.stats?.vision)||0)*1.05;}
function enemyCanObserve(game,target){
  const observers=[];
  for(const u of game.units||[]) if(u.alive&&u.team==='enemy'&&!u.embarkedIn) observers.push(u);
  for(const b of game.buildings||[]) if(b.alive&&b.team==='enemy'&&b.buildProgress>=0.85) observers.push(b);
  for(const o of observers){const r=detectionRadius(o);if(r>0&&dist(o,target)<=r)return true;}
  return false;
}

    cleanupDeadObjects() {

    }

    updateCamera(dt) {
      const keys = this.input.keys;
      const pitch = clamp(this.camera.pitch || 0.58, 0.30, 0.88);
      const speed = 1040 / Math.max(0.22, this.camera.zoom);
      let sx = 0;
      let sy = 0;
      if (keys.has('KeyW') || keys.has('ArrowUp')) sy -= 1;
      if (keys.has('KeyS') || keys.has('ArrowDown')) sy += 1;
      if (keys.has('KeyA') || keys.has('ArrowLeft')) sx -= 1;
      if (keys.has('KeyD') || keys.has('ArrowRight')) sx += 1;

      const mouse = this.input.mouse;
      const edge = 10;
      if (mouse.inside && !this.input.pointerDown && !this.isPointerOverHud(mouse.x, mouse.y)) {
        if (mouse.x < edge) sx -= 1;
        if (mouse.x > this.viewport.width - edge) sx += 1;
        if (mouse.y < edge) sy -= 1;
        if (mouse.y > this.viewport.height - edge) sy += 1;
      }

      if (sx || sy) {
        const length = Math.hypot(sx, sy) || 1;
        sx /= length;
        sy /= length;
        const c = Math.cos(this.camera.yaw || 0);
        const s = Math.sin(this.camera.yaw || 0);
        const planeY = sy / Math.max(0.34, pitch);
        const wx = c * sx + s * planeY;
        const wy = -s * sx + c * planeY;
        const worldLength = Math.hypot(wx, wy) || 1;
        this.camera.x += wx / worldLength * speed * dt;
        this.camera.y += wy / worldLength * speed * dt;
      }

      if (keys.has('BracketLeft')) this.camera.yaw -= dt * 0.92;

    enemyDetectorNear(unit) {

    findHealTarget(healer) {
      let best = null;
      let bestRatio = 1;
      const range = healer.stats.healRange + 110;
      const candidates = [
        ...this.querySpatial(this.unitSpatial, healer.x, healer.y, range),
        ...this.querySpatial(this.buildingSpatial, healer.x, healer.y, range)
      ];
      for (const entity of candidates) {
        if (!entity.alive || entity.team !== healer.team || entity === healer || entity.hp >= entity.maxHp) continue;
        const valid = healer.stats.healsMechanical ? (entity.kind === 'building' || entity.vehicle) : entity.infantry;
        if (!valid || distance(entity, healer) > healer.stats.healRange + entity.radius) continue;
        const ratio = entity.hp / entity.maxHp;
        if (ratio < bestRatio) { best = entity; bestRatio = ratio; }
      }
      return best;
    }

    findNearestResource(x, y, excludedId = null) {
      let best = null;
      let bestD2 = Infinity;
      for (const node of this.resources) {

        const d2 = (node.x - x) ** 2 + (node.y - y) ** 2;
        if (d2 < bestD2) { best = node; bestD2 = d2; }
      }
      return best;
    }

    findNearestDropoff(x, y, team) {

        if (d2 < bestD2) { best = building; bestD2 = d2; }
      }

    }

    hitTest(worldX, worldY, selectableOnly = true) {
      const candidates = [];
      for (const unit of this.units) {
        if (!unit.alive) continue;
        if (unit.team === 'enemy' && !this.isTargetableBy(unit, 'player')) continue;
        const d2 = (unit.x - worldX) ** 2 + (unit.y - worldY) ** 2;
        const hitRadius = unit.radius + 7 / this.camera.zoom;
        if (d2 <= hitRadius * hitRadius) candidates.push({ entity: unit, priority: 3, d2 });
      }
      for (const building of this.buildings) {
        if (!building.alive) continue;
        if (building.team === 'enemy' && !this.isVisibleAt(building.x, building.y)) continue;
        if (building.team === 'neutral' && !this.isExploredAt(building.x, building.y)) continue;
        const d2 = (building.x - worldX) ** 2 + (building.y - worldY) ** 2;
        if (d2 <= (building.radius + 5) ** 2) candidates.push({ entity: building, priority: 2, d2 });
      }
      if (!selectableOnly) {
        for (const resource of this.resources) {
          if (!resource.alive || !this.isExploredAt(resource.x, resource.y)) continue;
          const d2 = (resource.x - worldX) ** 2 + (resource.y - worldY) ** 2;
          if (d2 <= (resource.radius + 8) ** 2) candidates.push({ entity: resource, priority: 1, d2 });
        }
      } else {
        for (const resource of this.resources) {
          if (!resource.alive || !this.isExploredAt(resource.x, resource.y)) continue;
          const d2 = (resource.x - worldX) ** 2 + (resource.y - worldY) ** 2;
          if (d2 <= (resource.radius + 8) ** 2) candidates.push({ entity: resource, priority: 1, d2 });
        }
      }
      candidates.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority || a.d2 - b.d2);
      return candidates[0]?.entity || null;
    }

        this.selected.push(entity);
      }
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    isBuildPlacementValid(typeId, x, y) {
      const stats = getBuildingStats(typeId, this.teams.player);
      if (x < stats.radius + 20 || y < stats.radius + 20 || x > WORLD.width - stats.radius - 20 || y > WORLD.height - stats.radius - 20) return false;
      if (!this.isVisibleAt(x, y)) return false;

      for (const resource of this.resources) {
        if (!resource.alive) continue;
        if (distance(resource, { x, y }) < resource.radius + stats.radius + 18) return false;
      }
      // Крупные скальные элементы относятся к ландшафту и блокируют фундамент.
      for (const feature of this.decorations || []) {
        if (feature.type !== 'rock' || feature.radius < 13) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(feature.x - x, feature.y - y) < feature.radius + stats.radius + 10) return false;
      }
      // Юниты не блокируют фундамент: перед размещением они освобождают контур.
      const nearFriendly = [...this.units, ...this.buildings].some((entity) => entity.alive && entity.team === 'player' && distance(entity, { x, y }) <= 980);
      return nearFriendly;
    }

    findConstructionClearancePoint(unit, x, y, buildingRadius, reserved = []) {
      const originalX = unit.x;
      const originalY = unit.y;
      let angle = Math.atan2(originalY - y, originalX - x);
      if (!Number.isFinite(angle) || Math.hypot(originalX - x, originalY - y) < 1) angle = ((hashString(unit.id) % 628) / 100);
      const minimum = buildingRadius + unit.radius + 24;
      for (let ring = 0; ring < 8; ring += 1) {
        const radius = minimum + ring * Math.max(22, unit.radius * 1.6);
        const samples = 20 + ring * 4;
        for (let i = 0; i < samples; i += 1) {
          const signed = i === 0 ? 0 : (i % 2 ? 1 : -1) * Math.ceil(i / 2);
          const a = angle + signed * (TWO_PI / samples);
          const px = clamp(x + Math.cos(a) * radius, unit.radius + 5, WORLD.width - unit.radius - 5);
          const py = clamp(y + Math.sin(a) * radius, unit.radius + 5, WORLD.height - unit.radius - 5);
          if (this.findBuildingCollision(px, py, unit.radius)) continue;
          if (this.resources.some((resource) => resource.alive && Math.hypot(resource.x - px, resource.y - py) < resource.radius + unit.radius + 8)) continue;
          if ((this.decorations || []).some((feature) => feature.type === 'rock' && feature.radius >= 13 && Math.hypot(feature.x - px, feature.y - py) < feature.radius + unit.radius + 3)) continue;
          let occupied = false;
          for (const slot of reserved) {
            if (Math.hypot(slot.x - px, slot.y - py) < slot.radius + unit.radius + 6) { occupied = true; break; }
          }
          if (!occupied) {
            for (const other of this.units) {
              if (!other.alive || other === unit || other.air) continue;
              // Units already inside the new foundation will be moved in this same pass;
              // units outside it reserve their actual space immediately.
              const otherInside = Math.hypot(other.x - x, other.y - y) < buildingRadius + other.radius + 16;
              if (!otherInside && Math.hypot(other.x - px, other.y - py) < other.radius + unit.radius + 7) { occupied = true; break; }
            }
          }
          if (!occupied) return { x: px, y: py, radius: unit.radius };
        }
      }
      return null;
    }

  Game.prototype.hydrate=function(data){

  // The harvester loop now works ONLY from a finished extraction building.
  Unit.prototype.processHarvest=function(command,dt){
    const isHauler=this.typeId==='worker'||this.typeId==='resourceTruck'||this.stats?.resourceHauler;
    if(!isHauler){this.finishCommand();return;}
    let source=this.game.getEntity(command.resourceId);
    if(source?.kind==='resource'){
      const ext=this.game.getEntity(source.extractorBuildingId); source=ext?.alive?ext:null; if(source) command.resourceId=source.id;
    }
    const sourceUsable=!!(source?.alive&&source.kind==='building'&&source.completed&&source.stats?.logisticsExtractor&&source.team===this.team);
    if(!sourceUsable&&this.cargo<=0){this.finishCommand();return;}
    if(!sourceUsable&&this.cargo>0){command.phase='return';}
    if(!command.phase) this.setHarvestPhase(command,this.cargo>0?'return':'toResource');
    if(command.phase==='toResource'){
      if(this.moveTowardInteraction(source,command,dt,'pickup')){this.setHarvestPhase(command,'collect');this.harvestTimer=this.stats.harvestTime||1.2;}
      return;
    }
    if(command.phase==='collect'){
      this.harvestTimer-=dt;if(this.harvestTimer>0)return;
      const free=Math.max(0,(this.stats.cargoCapacity||700)-this.cargo);
      const available=Math.max(0,source.resourceBuffer83||0);
      const taken=Math.min(free,available);
      if(taken>0){source.resourceBuffer83-=taken;this.cargo+=taken;this.setHarvestPhase(command,'return');}
      else{this.harvestTimer=.8;}
      return;
    }
    if(command.phase==='return'){
      let drop=this.game.getEntity(command.dropoffId);
      if(!drop?.alive||!drop.completed||drop.team!==this.team||!drop.stats.dropoff){drop=this.game.findNearestDropoff(this.x,this.y,this.team);if(drop){command.dropoffId=drop.id;this.clearInteractionState(command);this.invalidateNavigation();}}
      if(!drop){this.finishCommand();return;}
      if(this.moveTowardInteraction(drop,command,dt,'dropoff')){
        if(this.cargo>0){const delivered=this.cargo;this.game.teams[this.team].credits+=delivered;if(this.team==='player')this.game.stats.resourcesCollected+=delivered;this.game.addEffect({type:'text',x:this.x,y:this.y-24,text:`+${Math.round(delivered)}`,color:'#ffe083',duration:1.1});this.cargo=0;}
        if(source?.alive&&source.completed){this.setHarvestPhase(command,'toResource');}else this.finishCommand();
      }
    }
  };

  UnitClass.prototype.fire = function(target) {
    const payment = canAffordAirShot126(this, target);
    if (!payment.allowed) return;
    const previousShot = this.lastShotAt;
    const previousCooldown = this.weaponCooldown || 0;
    const result = baseUnitFire126.call(this, target);
    if (payment.cost > 0 && (this.lastShotAt !== previousShot || (this.weaponCooldown || 0) > previousCooldown + .001)) {
      this.game.recordAirEngagementSpend126(this, payment.cost);
    }
    return result;
  };

  const baseBuildingFire126 = BuildingClass.prototype.fire;
  BuildingClass.prototype.fire = function(target) {
    if (!this.game.isStationaryDefensePowered(this)) return;

  TacticalAIClass.prototype.rememberEntity = function(entity) {
    const result = baseRememberEntity126.call(this, entity);
    const intel = entity?.id ? this.intel?.get(entity.id) : null;
    if (intel && entity.team === 'player') {
      intel.cargo = Number(entity.cargo) || 0;
      intel.cargoCapacity = Number(entity.stats?.cargoCapacity) || 0;
      intel.logisticsExtractor = Boolean(entity.stats?.logisticsExtractor);
      if (entity.typeId === 'resourceTruck') intel.value = Math.max(intel.value || 0, 300 + intel.cargo * .08);

      else if (entity.stats?.logisticsExtractor) intel.value = Math.max(intel.value || 0, 235);
    }
    return result;
  };

      this.regenTimer += dt;
      if (this.regenTimer >= this.regenDelay) {
        this.amount = Math.min(this.maxAmount, this.amount + this.regenRate * dt);
      }
    }

      this.amount -= taken;
      this.regenTimer = 0;
      if (this.amount <= 0 && this.regenRate <= 0) this.alive = false;
      return taken;
    }

      return {
        ...this.serializeBase(), kind: this.kind, amount: this.amount, maxAmount: this.maxAmount,
        variant: this.variant, radius: this.radius, regenRate: this.regenRate,
        regenDelay: this.regenDelay, regenTimer: this.regenTimer
      };

  class Building extends Entity {
    constructor(game, data) {
      const teamState = game.teams[data.team] || game.teams.neutral;
      const stats = getBuildingStats(data.typeId, teamState);
      const construction = data.construction ?? 1;
      super(game, {
        ...data,
        hp: data.hp ?? stats.hp * Math.max(0.05, construction),
        maxHp: data.maxHp ?? stats.hp,
        radius: stats.radius,
        armor: 'building',
        vision: stats.vision,
        detector: stats.detector || 0
      });
      this.kind = 'building';
      this.typeId = data.typeId;
      this.stats = stats;
      this.construction = clamp(construction, 0, 1);
      if (this.construction < 1) this.hp = Math.min(this.hp, this.maxHp * Math.max(0.05, this.construction));
      this.autoConstruct = Boolean(data.autoConstruct);
      this.queue = Array.isArray(data.queue) ? data.queue : [];
      this.rallyPoint = data.rallyPoint || { x: this.x + (this.team === 'player' ? 120 : -120), y: this.y };
      this.weaponCooldown = data.weaponCooldown || 0;
      this.rotation = Number.isFinite(data.rotation) ? data.rotation : (this.team === 'player' ? 0 : Math.PI);
      this.weaponRotation = Number.isFinite(data.weaponRotation) ? data.weaponRotation : this.rotation;
      this.desiredWeaponRotation = Number.isFinite(data.desiredWeaponRotation) ? data.desiredWeaponRotation : this.weaponRotation;
      this.weaponTargetId = data.weaponTargetId || null;
      this.shotSequence = data.shotSequence || 0;
      this.recoil = data.recoil || 0;
      this.incomeTimer = data.incomeTimer || 0;
      this.auraTimer = data.auraTimer || 0;
      this.captureProgress = data.captureProgress || 0;
      this.sabotagedUntil = data.sabotagedUntil || 0;
      this.compromisedUntil = data.compromisedUntil || 0;
      this.interceptCooldown = data.interceptCooldown || 0;
      this.sabotagePulse = data.sabotagePulse || 0;
      this.completedAnnounced = construction >= 1;
    }
    get completed() { return this.construction >= 1; }
    heal(amount) {
      if (!this.alive || amount <= 0) return 0;
      const constructionCap = this.completed ? this.maxHp : this.maxHp * Math.max(0.05, this.construction);
      const before = this.hp;
      this.hp = Math.min(constructionCap, this.hp + amount);
      return this.hp - before;
    }
    addConstruction(amount) {
      if (!this.alive || this.completed) return;
      const previous = this.construction;
      this.construction = clamp(this.construction + amount, 0, 1);
      const hpGain = this.maxHp * (this.construction - previous);
      const constructionCap = this.maxHp * Math.max(0.05, this.construction);
      this.hp = Math.min(constructionCap, this.hp + hpGain);
      if (this.completed) this.completeConstruction();
    }
    completeConstruction() {
      if (this.completedAnnounced) return;
      this.completedAnnounced = true;
      this.construction = 1;
      this.hp = Math.max(this.hp, this.maxHp * 0.62);
      this.game.recalculatePower();

        this.game.sound.build();
      }
      this.game.addCommandXp(this.team, Math.max(80, this.stats.cost * 0.12));

    update(dt) {
      if (!this.alive) return;
      this.weaponCooldown = Math.max(0, this.weaponCooldown - dt);
      this.interceptCooldown = Math.max(0, this.interceptCooldown - dt);
      this.recoil = Math.max(0, this.recoil - dt * 5.8);
      this.captureProgress = Math.max(0, this.captureProgress - dt * 0.03);

      if (!this.completed) {
        if (this.autoConstruct) this.addConstruction(dt / Math.max(1, this.stats.time));
        return;
      }

      if (this.sabotagedUntil > this.game.time) {
        this.sabotagePulse -= dt;
        if (this.sabotagePulse <= 0) {
          this.sabotagePulse = 0.45 + this.game.rng.next() * 0.55;
          this.game.addEffect({ type: 'smoke', x: this.x + (this.game.rng.next() - 0.5) * this.radius, y: this.y - this.radius * 0.25, radius: 18 + this.game.rng.next() * 16, duration: 1.7 });

        }
        return;
      }

      if (this.stats.income && this.team !== 'neutral') {
        const interval = Math.max(0.25, this.stats.incomeInterval || 1);
        this.incomeTimer += dt * Math.max(0.25, this.game.teams[this.team].powerFactor);
        while (this.incomeTimer >= interval) {
          this.incomeTimer -= interval;
          this.game.teams[this.team].credits += this.stats.income;
          if (this.team === 'player') this.game.stats.resourcesCollected += this.stats.income;
        }
      }

      if (this.stats.healAura || this.stats.repairAura) {
        this.auraTimer -= dt;
        if (this.auraTimer <= 0) {
          const pulse = 0.25;
          this.auraTimer = pulse;
          this.updateAuras(pulse);
        }
      }

      if (this.queue.length) this.updateQueue(dt);
      if (this.stats.weapon) this.updateWeapon(dt);
    }

    heal(amount) {
      if (!this.alive || amount <= 0) return 0;
      const before = this.hp;
      this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + amount);
      return this.hp - before;
    }
    takeDamage(rawDamage, source = null, damageType = null) {
      if (!this.alive || rawDamage <= 0) return;
      let damage = rawDamage;
      if (damageType && damageType.bonus) damage *= damageType.bonus[this.armor] ?? 1;
      const intrinsicReduction = clamp(this.stats?.damageReduction || 0, 0, 0.55);
      const shieldReduction = this.game.getShieldReduction ? this.game.getShieldReduction(this) : 0;
      damage *= 1 - clamp(intrinsicReduction + shieldReduction, 0, 0.68);
      this.hp -= damage;
      this.lastDamagedAt = this.game.time;
      if (this.stats?.covertOps) {
        this.compromisedUntil = Math.max(this.compromisedUntil || 0, this.game.time + 12);
        this.coverIntegrity = Math.max(0, (this.coverIntegrity ?? this.stats.coverIntegrity ?? 0.8) - 0.18);
      }
      if (source && source.team !== this.team && source instanceof Unit) source.gainExperience(damage * 0.42);
      if (this.hp <= 0) this.die(source);
    }
    die(source = null) {
      if (!this.alive) return;
      this.alive = false;
      this.selected = false;
      this.game.handleDeath(this, source);
    }
    serializeBase() {
      return {
        id: this.id, team: this.team, x: this.x, y: this.y, alive: this.alive,
        hp: this.hp, maxHp: this.maxHp
      };
    }
  }

  class ResourceNode extends Entity {
    constructor(game, data) {
      super(game, { ...data, team: 'neutral', radius: data.radius || 46, hp: 1, maxHp: 1 });
      this.kind = 'resource';
      this.amount = data.amount ?? 120000;
      this.maxAmount = data.maxAmount ?? this.amount;
      this.variant = data.variant || 'crystal';
      this.regenRate = data.regenRate || 0;
      this.regenDelay = data.regenDelay || 8;
      this.regenTimer = data.regenTimer || 0;
    }
    update(dt) {
      if (!this.alive || this.regenRate <= 0 || this.amount >= this.maxAmount) return;
      this.regenTimer += dt;
      if (this.regenTimer >= this.regenDelay) {
        this.amount = Math.min(this.maxAmount, this.amount + this.regenRate * dt);
      }
    }
    extract(amount) {
      const taken = Math.min(this.amount, amount);
      this.amount -= taken;
      this.regenTimer = 0;
      if (this.amount <= 0 && this.regenRate <= 0) this.alive = false;
      return taken;
    }
    serialize() {
      return {
        ...this.serializeBase(), kind: this.kind, amount: this.amount, maxAmount: this.maxAmount,
        variant: this.variant, radius: this.radius, regenRate: this.regenRate,
        regenDelay: this.regenDelay, regenTimer: this.regenTimer
      };
    }
  }

  const baseHydrate80 = Game.prototype.hydrate;
  Game.prototype.hydrate = function(data) {

    this.resourceExtractorMode80 = true;
    for (const b of this.buildings) {

  Game.prototype.findResourceAnchor80 = function(typeId, x, y) {
    const stats = BUILDING_TYPES[typeId];
    if (!stats?.placeOnResource) return null;
    const variants = Array.isArray(stats.placeOnResource) ? stats.placeOnResource : [stats.placeOnResource];
    let best = null;
    for (const node of this.resources) {
      if (!node.alive || !variants.includes(node.variant) || !this.isVisibleAt(node.x, node.y)) continue;
      if (node.extractorBuildingId && this.getEntity(node.extractorBuildingId)?.alive) continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(node.x - x, node.y - y);
      if (d > Math.max(node.radius + 54, 110)) continue;
      if (!best || d < best.d) best = { node, d };
    }
    return best?.node || null;
  };

  // Unit definitions are immutable during play. Previously every constructed
  // soldier cloned the whole definition, weapon, target list and bonus table.
  // A 10k-v-10k battle therefore retained twenty thousand identical object
  // graphs and eventually paid for a long compacting GC. Share one resolved

  const TEAM_UNIT_STATS_CACHE = new WeakMap();

  function getUnitStats(typeId, teamState) {
    const base = UNIT_TYPES[typeId];
    if (!base) throw new Error(`Неизвестный тип юнита: ${typeId}`);
    const upgrades = teamState.upgrades;
    let cache = TEAM_UNIT_STATS_CACHE.get(teamState);
    if (!cache || cache.faction !== teamState.faction || cache.upgrades !== upgrades || cache.upgradeCount !== upgrades.size) {
      cache = { faction: teamState.faction, upgrades, upgradeCount: upgrades.size, types: new Map() };
      TEAM_UNIT_STATS_CACHE.set(teamState, cache);
    }
    const cached = cache.types.get(typeId);
    if (cached) return cached;
    const faction = FACTIONS[teamState.faction];
    const stats = {
      ...base,
      requires: base.requires ? [...base.requires] : [],
      weapon: cloneWeapon(base.weapon)
    };
    stats.cost = Math.round(base.cost * faction.cost);
    stats.time = base.time * faction.build;
    stats.hp = Math.round(base.hp * faction.hp);
    stats.speed = base.speed * faction.speed;
    if (stats.weapon) stats.weapon.damage *= faction.damage;
    if (teamState.upgrades.has('munitions') && stats.weapon) stats.weapon.damage *= 1.15;
    if (teamState.upgrades.has('armor') && stats.vehicle) stats.hp = Math.round(stats.hp * 1.15);
    if (teamState.upgrades.has('targeting')) {
      stats.vision *= 1.10;
      if (stats.weapon) stats.weapon.range *= 1.10;
    }
    if (teamState.upgrades.has('reactiveArmor') && !stats.air) stats.hp = Math.round(stats.hp * 1.12);
    if (teamState.upgrades.has('siegeCalibration') && stats.weapon?.ballistic) {
      stats.weapon.damage *= 1.16;
--- ammo #2 offset=98460 line=1632 ---
        this.amount = Math.min(this.maxAmount, this.amount + this.regenRate * dt);
      }
    }
    extract(amount) {
      const taken = Math.min(this.amount, amount);
      this.amount -= taken;
      this.regenTimer = 0;
      if (this.amount <= 0 && this.regenRate <= 0) this.alive = false;
      return taken;
    }
    serialize() {
      return {
        ...this.serializeBase(), kind: this.kind, amount: this.amount, maxAmount: this.maxAmount,
        variant: this.variant, radius: this.radius, regenRate: this.regenRate,
        regenDelay: this.regenDelay, regenTimer: this.regenTimer
      };
    }
  }

  class Unit extends Entity {
    constructor(game, data) {
      const teamState = game.teams[data.team];
      const stats = getUnitStats(data.typeId, teamState);
      super(game, {
        ...data,
        hp: data.hp ?? stats.hp,
        maxHp: data.maxHp ?? stats.hp,
        radius: stats.radius,
        armor: stats.armor,
        vision: stats.vision,
        detector: stats.detector || 0
      });
      this.kind = 'unit';
      this.typeId = data.typeId;
      this.stats = stats;
      this.rotation = data.rotation ?? (this.team === 'player' ? 0 : Math.PI);
      this.commandQueue = Array.isArray(data.commandQueue) ? data.commandQueue : [];
      this.weaponCooldown = data.weaponCooldown || 0;
      this.healCooldown = 0;
      this.cargo = data.cargo || 0;
      this.harvestTimer = data.harvestTimer || 0;
      this.xp = data.xp || 0;
      this.rank = data.rank ?? stats.startingRank ?? 1;
      if (data.maxHp == null && this.rank > 1) {
        this.maxHp *= 1 + (this.rank - 1) * 0.12;
        this.hp = this.maxHp;
      }
      this.kills = data.kills || 0;
      this.air = Boolean(stats.air);
      // v7.4: fixed-wing aircraft carry finite ordnance/fuel and can return to an airfield for service.
      this.airAmmo = Number.isFinite(data.airAmmo) ? data.airAmmo : null;
      this.airAmmoMax = Number.isFinite(data.airAmmoMax) ? data.airAmmoMax : null;

  class Unit extends Entity {

  function v74FixedWing(unit) {
    return Boolean(unit?.alive && unit.air && unit.stats?.mobilityClass === 'fixedWing');
  }

  function v74AirProfile(unit) {
    const role = unit.game?.unitVisualRole ? unit.game.unitVisualRole(unit) : (unit.stats.visualRole || '');
    const specifiedCapacity = Number(unit.stats.sortieAmmoMax);
    if (Number.isFinite(specifiedCapacity)) return { capacity: specifiedCapacity, unitCost: unit.stats.sortieAmmoUnitCost || 90 };
    if (!unit.stats.weapon) return { capacity: 0, unitCost: 0 };
    if (role === 'interceptor') return { capacity: 10, unitCost: 58 };
    if (role === 'multirole') return { capacity: 8, unitCost: 76 };
    if (role === 'bomber') return { capacity: 6, unitCost: 145 };
    if (role === 'heavyBomber') return { capacity: 5, unitCost: 205 };
    if (role === 'stealthStriker') return { capacity: 6, unitCost: 132 };
    if (role === 'aerialArtillery') return { capacity: 7, unitCost: 108 };
    if (role === 'aeroBallisticCarrier') return { capacity: 0, unitCost: 0 };
    return { capacity: 8, unitCost: 82 };
  }

  function v74EnsureAircraftLogistics(unit) {
    if (!v74FixedWing(unit)) return false;
    const profile = v74AirProfile(unit);
    if (!Number.isFinite(unit.airAmmoMax)) unit.airAmmoMax = profile.capacity;
    if (!Number.isFinite(unit.airAmmo)) unit.airAmmo = unit.airAmmoMax;
    unit.airAmmo = clamp(unit.airAmmo, 0, unit.airAmmoMax);
    if (!Number.isFinite(unit.airAmmoUnitCost)) unit.airAmmoUnitCost = profile.unitCost;
    const fuelBase = 145 + (hashString(unit.id) % 61); // 145–205 seconds per sortie.
    if (!Number.isFinite(unit.sortieFuelMax)) unit.sortieFuelMax = fuelBase;
    if (!Number.isFinite(unit.sortieFuel)) unit.sortieFuel = unit.sortieFuelMax;
    if (!Array.isArray(unit.airSavedCommands)) unit.airSavedCommands = [];
    return true;
  }

  Game.prototype.isStationaryDefensePowered = function(building) {
    if (!building?.alive || building.kind !== 'building') return true;
    if (building.team !== 'player' && building.team !== 'enemy') return false;
    const team = this.teams[building.team];
    if (!team) return false;
    if (building.sabotagedUntil > this.time || !building.completed) return false;
    // Power factor below 60% represents load shedding; fixed defensive electronics are disconnected first.
    return team.powerProduced > 0 && team.powerFactor >= 0.60;
  };

  Game.prototype.findOperationalAirfield = function(teamKey, x, y) {
    const team = this.teams[teamKey];
    const fields = this.buildings.filter((building) => building.alive && building.completed && building.team === teamKey &&
      ['airfield', 'advancedAirfield'].includes(building.typeId) && building.sabotagedUntil <= this.time);
    if (!fields.length) return null;
    // Airfields can still receive an emergency landing in brownout, but powered fields service faster.
    return fields.sort((a, b) => {
      const ap = team?.powerProduced > 0 && team?.powerFactor >= 0.38 ? 0 : 700;
      const bp = ap;
      return Math.hypot(a.x - x, a.y - y) + ap - (Math.hypot(b.x - x, b.y - y) + bp);
    })[0] || null;
  };

  Unit.prototype.requestAirService = function(reason = 'боезапас') {
    if (!v74EnsureAircraftLogistics(this) || this.airServiceState) return false;
    const field = this.game.findOperationalAirfield(this.team, this.x, this.y);

  function normalizeOperationalMobility(stats, typeId) {
    const role = stats.visualRole || UNIT_VISUAL_ROLES[stats.archetypeIndex] || '';
    if (stats.air) {
      const isDrone = ['reconDrone', 'repairDrone'].includes(role) || /drone/i.test(typeId);
      const isHelicopter = role === 'lightGunship' || typeId === 'helicopter';
      const minimum = isDrone ? 245 : isHelicopter ? 275 : 330;
      const maximum = isDrone ? 390 : isHelicopter ? 420 : role.includes('Bomber') || role === 'bomber' ? 470 : 560;
      stats.speed = clamp(stats.speed * 1.78, minimum, maximum);
      stats.radius = Math.max(8.2, stats.radius * 0.64);
      stats.visualScale = 0.91;
      stats.mobilityClass = isDrone ? 'drone' : isHelicopter ? 'rotary' : 'fixedWing';
    } else if (stats.vehicle) {
      const isScout = ['reconCar', 'assaultCar', 'commandVehicle'].includes(role) || typeId === 'scout' || typeId === 'mobileFireGroup';
      const isHeavy = ['mainTank', 'tankHunter', 'siegeTank', 'superHeavy', 'strategicMissileTEL'].includes(role) || ['tank', 'juggernaut'].includes(typeId);
      const isArtillery = ['howitzer', 'rocketArtillery', 'tacticalMissileTEL', 'cruiseMissileTEL'].includes(role) || typeId === 'artillery';
      const isSupport = ['mobileAA', 'repairVehicle', 'droneLauncherTEL'].includes(role) || ['aa', 'counterUASVehicle', 'longRangeSAM'].includes(typeId);
      const minimum = isScout ? 128 : isArtillery ? 82 : isHeavy ? 88 : isSupport ? 96 : 104;
      const maximum = isScout ? 215 : isArtillery ? 122 : isHeavy ? 132 : 168;
      const multiplier = isScout ? 1.22 : isArtillery ? 1.54 : isHeavy ? 1.34 : 1.30;
      stats.speed = clamp(stats.speed * multiplier, minimum, maximum);
      stats.radius = Math.max(9.4, stats.radius * 0.62);
      stats.visualScale = 0.90;
      stats.mobilityClass = isScout ? 'wheeledFast' : isArtillery ? 'trackedArtillery' : isHeavy ? 'trackedHeavy' : 'groundVehicle';
    } else if (stats.infantry || stats.armor === 'infantry') {
      const isHeavy = ['exoInfantry', 'heroInfantry'].includes(role);
      const isScout = ['sniper', 'infiltrator'].includes(role) || typeId === 'saboteur' || typeId === 'commando';
      stats.speed = clamp(stats.speed * (isScout ? 0.60 : isHeavy ? 0.48 : 0.54), isHeavy ? 38 : 43, isScout ? 76 : 68);
      // The engineer uses a vehicle-class gameplay radius, while the approved
      // narrow human atlas provides the exact visible click silhouette.  A
      // radius of 17 matches the resolved main-tank radius without turning one
      // worker into an oversized obstacle or a screen-high selection column.
      stats.radius = typeId === 'worker'
        ? 17
        : Math.max(isHeavy ? 6.4 : 5.0, stats.radius * (isHeavy ? 0.44 : 0.385));
      stats.visualScale = typeId === 'worker' ? 0.90 : isHeavy ? 0.84 : 0.78;
      stats.mobilityClass = isHeavy ? 'heavyInfantry' : 'infantry';
    }
    stats.turnRate = stats.air ? 2.8 : stats.vehicle ? (stats.mobilityClass === 'wheeledFast' ? 4.6 : 3.1) : 7.2;
    return stats;
  }

  class ResourceNode extends Entity {
    constructor(game, data) {
      super(game, { ...data, team: 'neutral', radius: data.radius || 46, hp: 1, maxHp: 1 });
      this.kind = 'resource';
      this.amount = data.amount ?? 120000;
      this.maxAmount = data.maxAmount ?? this.amount;
      this.variant = data.variant || 'crystal';
      this.regenRate = data.regenRate || 0;
      this.regenDelay = data.regenDelay || 8;
      this.regenTimer = data.regenTimer || 0;
    }
    update(dt) {
      if (!this.alive || this.regenRate <= 0 || this.amount >= this.maxAmount) return;
      this.regenTimer += dt;
      if (this.regenTimer >= this.regenDelay) {
        this.amount = Math.min(this.maxAmount, this.amount + this.regenRate * dt);
      }
    }
    extract(amount) {
      const taken = Math.min(this.amount, amount);
      this.amount -= taken;
      this.regenTimer = 0;
      if (this.amount <= 0 && this.regenRate <= 0) this.alive = false;
      return taken;
    }
    serialize() {
      return {
        ...this.serializeBase(), kind: this.kind, amount: this.amount, maxAmount: this.maxAmount,
        variant: this.variant, radius: this.radius, regenRate: this.regenRate,
        regenDelay: this.regenDelay, regenTimer: this.regenTimer
      };
    }
  }

  const baseHydrate80 = Game.prototype.hydrate;
  Game.prototype.hydrate = function(data) {
    baseHydrate80.call(this, data);
    this.resourceExtractorMode80 = true;
    for (const b of this.buildings) {
      if (b?.stats?.extractor && !b.resourceNodeId) {
        const match = this.resources.filter(r => r.alive && r.variant === b.stats.placeOnResource).sort((a,b2)=>dist80(a,b)-dist80(b2,b))[0];
        if (match && dist80(match, b) < 120) { b.resourceNodeId = match.id; match.extractorBuildingId = b.id; }
      }
    }
  };

  Game.prototype.findResourceAnchor80 = function(typeId, x, y) {
    const stats = BUILDING_TYPES[typeId];
    if (!stats?.placeOnResource) return null;
    const variants = Array.isArray(stats.placeOnResource) ? stats.placeOnResource : [stats.placeOnResource];
    let best = null;
    for (const node of this.resources) {
      if (!node.alive || !variants.includes(node.variant) || !this.isVisibleAt(node.x, node.y)) continue;
      if (node.extractorBuildingId && this.getEntity(node.extractorBuildingId)?.alive) continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(node.x - x, node.y - y);
      if (d > Math.max(node.radius + 54, 110)) continue;
      if (!best || d < best.d) best = { node, d };
    }
    return best?.node || null;
  };

  const basePlacementValid80 = Game.prototype.isBuildPlacementValid;
  Game.prototype.isBuildPlacementValid = function(typeId, x, y) {
    const stats = BUILDING_TYPES[typeId];
    if (stats?.placeOnResource) {
      const node = this.findResourceAnchor80(typeId, x, y);
      if (!node) return false;
      if (this.collidesWithBuilding(node.x, node.y, (stats.radius || 30) + 6)) return false;
      return true;
    }
    return basePlacementValid80.call(this, typeId, x, y);
  };
